# Grandma
Every story in which Grandma speaks.

### Attic: These would make lovely kindling!
`AtticCharacterTask1` · seen 25.04.02–26.07.01
- trigger: area, area=Attic, areaId=Attic, hotspotId=AtticCharacterTask1, phase=task appears, task=These would make lovely kindling! (versions 25.04.02–26.07.01)

**MADDIE** (Encouraging): Well... time to get rid of all this old junk.
**GRANDMA** (Surprised): Junk?! Dearie, if I thought it was just junk, I wouldn’t have put it in the attic, would I?
**MADDIE** (Doubtful): Grandma! You snuck up on me! Well, most of this stuff is worthless. Besides, I wouldn’t mind never seeing some of it again!
**GRANDMA** (Relaxed): I understand, dearie. But perhaps, instead of just throwing it away, you might find it a new home?
**GRANDMA** (Encouraging): Everything deserves a second chance, right?
**MADDIE** (Worried): I... I guess you’re right Grandma.

### Attic: These shards would make lovely jewelry.
`AtticCharacterTask2` · seen 25.04.02–26.07.01
- trigger: area, area=Attic, areaId=Attic, hotspotId=AtticCharacterTask10, phase=task completed, task=These shards would make lovely jewelry. (versions 25.04.02–26.07.01)

**MADDIE** (Frustrated): Grandma, a lot of these things are just broken pieces!
**GRANDMA** (Joyous): Ah, I remember this... It belonged to your mother. She kept the pieces, hoping to make a necklace one day.
**GRANDMA** (Discouraged): I guess she never got around to it...
**MADDIE** (Encouraging): Well, I’m sure someone will! Let’s make sure these get donated! They are very pretty, after all!

### Pool House Patio: Open jammed Patio door
`BBQArea_Intro` · seen 23.09.02–26.07.01
- trigger: area, area=Pool House Patio, areaId=BBQArea, hotspotId=PoolHouseOpenBBQArea, phase=task completed, task=Open jammed Patio door (versions 24.04.01–26.07.01)

**MADDIE**: You wanted to see me, Grandma? What's going on?
**MADDIE** (Worried): Is this about your arrest?! Are you ready to talk about it?
**GRANDMA** (Worried): Uh, no. I just want to tell you some things about your family before it's too late.
**MADDIE** (Surprised): Too late for what, Grandma?
**GRANDMA**: Never you mind. But you see this place? Years ago it was just an empty wasteland.
**GRANDMA** (Encouraging): Your grandfather turned it into a BBQ area for me as a gift on our tenth wedding anniversary.
**GRANDMA** (Encouraging): We hosted a big BBQ party here then. Everyone we knew was present. Just like when we got married ten years earlier.
**MADDIE** (Joyous): Well, we should fix it up! I promise that I'll make it look beautiful for you.

### Pool House Patio: Clean pavement
`BBQArea_Outro` · seen 23.09.02–26.07.01
- trigger: area, area=Pool House Patio, areaId=BBQArea, hotspotId=BBQAreaSidePathWashPavement, phase=task completed, task=Clean pavement (versions 24.04.01–26.07.01)

**GRANDMA** (Encouraging): Maddie, you've outdone yourself! It's as if you've turned back time.
**MADDIE**: I'm glad you like it. Will you tell me something else about Grandpa Charlie?
**GRANDMA**: He was a real romantic, giving me flowers, taking me out to the movies.
**GRANDMA** (Worried): But then I lost him, and I've spent years missing him.
**MADDIE** (Worried): I'm sorry, Grandma. Did your arrest have anything to do with him?
**GRANDMA** (Frustrated): I don't want to talk about that! Just... let's get back to gardening and forget it.

### BeachRightDiscovered
`BeachRightDiscovered` · seen 22.02.06–26.07.01

**MADDIE** (Encouraging): What was this space all about? It's nothing like a grand mansion.
**GRANDMA** (Thinking): This was where Skatie Katie and Hang Ten hung out. Their little slice of paradise.
**MADDIE** (Surprised): Skatie, Hang.. Grandma, are you feeling okay? This is making less and less sense.
**GRANDMA** (Doubtful): Katie "Skatie" Boulton, my dear older cousin. She fell madly in love with Casey "Hang Ten" Schultz.  
  _(1 cosmetic edit not shown)_
**MADDIE** (Frustrated): You have a cousin? Grandma, now you have to tell me everything!
**GRANDMA**: Not now, honey, the cat’s waiting for her bath.
**MADDIE** (Worried): If I ever needed a reason to think my family was weird...

### BeachRightTotemFinished
`BeachRightTotemFinished` · seen 22.02.06–26.07.01

**MADDIE** (Encouraging): Alright, mind blown! So he was a surfer, she was a roller skater, and they came together and made skateboards?
**GRANDMA** (Surprised): That's right, sweetie. Cousin Katie always said they invented the skateboard.
**MADDIE**: Invented the skateboard? You're kidding, right?
**GRANDMA**: I don't know about that, but every surfer who came here looking for Casey showed up with a surfboard and left with a skateboard..
**MADDIE** (Worried): This makes sense after all! Grandma, for a moment I was worried about you. So, I had this question about..
**GRANDMA** (Doubtful): Oh my gosh! I left the bath running! And I put an extra dollop of bubbles in!
**MADDIE** (Surprised): But you don’t have a bath!

### BeachRightWorkshopCleanedUp
`BeachRightWorkshopCleanedUp` · seen 22.02.06–26.07.01

**MADDIE**: What's with all of these surfboards?
**GRANDMA** (Surprised): Casey was one of the most famous surfers back in the day. He was looking for big waves. He was a legend.
**MADDIE** (Relaxed): But there are no waves here. This is the calmest part of the sea I know!
**GRANDMA** (Worried): Katie and Casey fell in love, and she brought him here. Casey was looking for the fabled Perfect Wave that never came.
**MADDIE**: There must be a happy ending in here somewhere, right?
**GRANDMA** (Discouraged): I can’t answer that right now! Not without first pouring myself a mint julep. Back in a swish!
**GRANDMA** (Doubtful): In fact, I don't think I want to talk about it right now. Sorry, Maddie.

**GRANDMA** (Charming): How is your investigation going? I thought you must be hungry by now, so I've brought you some apple pie fresh from the oven!
**RODDY** (Encouraging): Thank you, I'm starving!
**JACKIE**: We are so close, but a piece seems to be missing. A lot of people had a potential motive, but everyone we interviewed had an alibi.
**JACKIE** (Thinking): The only other person who came by her house that day was the mailman. Apparently, a package addressed to you was mistakenly delivered to her house.
**GRANDMA** (Surprised): Very mysterious. Wait, did you say a package was delivered? Let me see the photos of the crime scene again!
**JACKIE** (Frustrated): Sure, but I'm not sure you'll find anything there. We've combed through them so many times.
**GRANDMA** (Thinking): The position of the body, the coloration of the skin...
**GRANDMA** (Annoyed): And the package was stamped in... *Sigh* It's not the first time I've encountered this.
**RODDY** (Surprised): What is it??
**GRANDMA** (Frustrated): Lady Voyance was always curious to a fault, but this time she stuck her nose in the wrong place. She was killed by a poison, delivered in that package.
**RODDY** (Worried): The package that was addressed to... you??
**GRANDMA** (Frustrated): I am afraid so. There are people who wish me ill, and this time Lady Voyance happened to be in the wrong place at the wrong time.
**JACKIE** (Thinking): So who's the killer?
**GRANDMA** (Doubtful): That, I am afraid, is classified. It wouldn't be safe to you or anyone here to know.
**RODDY** (Worried): *Gulp*
**GRANDMA**: Now help yourselves to some apple pie before it gets cold! I have a phone call to make.
